Owner offered targeted driving assistance (laps, selected cars and wall impacts). Recorded [owner-assisted test protocol](docs/OWNER_ASSISTED_TESTING.md): prepare/verify capture first, give one precise maneuver, hand input exclusively to the owner, verify evidence before repeating, and separate handling feedback from measured state. This can help geometric-contact and longer-sequence research; it is not a completed test or evidence of repeatability. No driving session requested now and no desktop input sent. Continue offline integration until a prepared experiment needs owner input.

Progress review requested by the owner: [dated assessment](docs/PROGRESS_ASSESSMENT_2026-10-09.md) separates established capabilities from missing integration, records blockers/risks and gives a subjective estimate of approximately 25% toward full single-player coverage (15–35% uncertainty range), or 40% toward the first timed lap (25–55%). These are planning judgments, not measured code coverage or delivery dates. No drivable Rust build exists. No hard blocker prevents offline work; complete vehicle timing/initialization, geometric contact production and application integration block the playable milestone. GRID window recovery remains a temporary live-capture limitation. Next priority: a complete Lacetti/Donington driving loop, with research tied to its missing dependencies. README and roadmap brought up to date. F147 research `a250e47` and implementation `226c033` are pushed to the owner's upstream. This review introduces no new compatibility or simulation validation claims.

Independent Rust/Bevy engine for Race Driver: GRID (2008), loading the player's purchased Steam resources. Static Lacetti, Donington surfaces and placed scenery render in Bevy 0.19.1. Entity startup visuals and track collision geometry are GPU-verified; binary XML and collision descriptors decode. Rust reproduces twelve live placement matrices exactly from recorded inputs; source-only alignment retains two one-float-step X differences. Original whole-query policy, render/body linkage, shader fidelity, collision simulation and driving remain ahead. [Live placement evidence](docs/LIVE_SPAWN_CAPTURE.md), [car viewer](docs/STATIC_VIEWER.md), [Donington scene](docs/DONINGTON_SCENE.md).

The owner has authorized reasonable analysis/decompilation and Rust rewrite work, including needed tool installation. All progress and findings must be recorded here or in linked Markdown research documents. This log is the durable project index, with evidence kept locally.

## Hypotheses and unresolved questions

- **H001, medium confidence:** the observed crash occurs in unsafe startup cleanup and may mask an earlier initialization failure. Do not call this the initiating root cause yet.
- Determine the first failed initialization operation before the apparent cleanup path.
- Establish whether CPU detection/worker scheduling, graphics-device creation, audio, overlays, an absent resource or another subsystem triggers that failure.
- An affinity limit may leave some CPU-count detection APIs unchanged; F008 does not rule out every CPU/worker-map defect.
- Lacetti/Donington pairing and route_0 reads are confirmed. Resolve the human-readable layout label, cached/shared dependencies and internal container consumption; do not treat the observed 130-file set as exhaustive.
- Identify the generic HID game controller and any wheel/pedal requirements when reference driving is available.
- **H002, provisional:** repeated display enumeration and/or instrumentation overhead may stretch startup well beyond the baseline. The short Procmon/native-debug probes do not establish which operation fails first.
- **H003, confirmed startup remedy in isolated copy:** process-codepage compatibility controls the observed startup failure. Paired manifest controls differ only in `en-US` versus `UTF-8` and show repeated survival versus the original exception (F018); owner confirms main menu (F019). The later `uk-UA` copy supports autosave persistence and driving. Exact initiating API/conversion and broader game-mode compatibility remain unresolved. No system-locale change/reboot made.
